									add_building_construction = { type = infrastructure level = 2 instant_build = yes }

										limit = { free_building_slots = { building = infrastructure size > 0 } }

									}
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				IF = {
					limit = { free_building_slots = { building = infrastructure size > 0 } }
					add_building_construction = { type = infrastructure level = 1 instant_build = yes }
				}
			}
		}

		available_if_capitulated = no
		will_lead_to_war_with = CAN
		search_filters = { FOCUS_FILTER_ANNEXATION  }

		completion_reward = {
			IF = {
				limit = { has_idea = AZR_AST_british_empire_4_idea }
				swap_ideas = {
					remove_idea = AZR_AST_british_empire_4_idea
					add_idea = AZR_AST_british_empire_5_idea
				}
				ELSE = {
					swap_ideas = {
						remove_idea = AZR_AST_british_empire_3_idea
						add_idea = AZR_AST_british_empire_4_idea
					}
				}
			}
			every_state = {
				limit = {
					is_core_of = CAN
					NOT = {
						OR = {
							is_claimed_by = ROOT
							is_core_of = ROOT
						}
					}
				}
				add_claim_by = ROOT
			}
			IF = {
				limit = { is_subject = yes }
				effect_tooltip = {
					ROOT = {
						create_wargoal = {
							type = annex_everything
							target = CAN
							expire = 0
						}
					}
				}
			}
			ELSE = {
				every_country = {
					limit = {
						NOT = {
							OR = {
								has_war_with = ROOT
								is_subject_of = ROOT
								is_in_faction_with = ROOT
							}
						}
						any_owned_state = { is_core_of = CAN }
					}
					IF = {
						limit = { original_tag = CAN }
						ROOT = {
							create_wargoal = {
								type = annex_everything
								target = PREV
								expire = 0
							}
						}
					}
					ELSE = {
						ROOT = {
							create_wargoal = {
								type = take_state_focus
								target = PREV
								expire = 0
							}
						}
					}
				}
				every_country = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							AND = {
								is_subject_of = ROOT
								any_owned_state = { is_core_of = CAN }
								NOT = { original_tag = CAN }
							}
							AND = {
								is_in_faction_with = ROOT
								any_owned_state = { is_core_of = CAN }
							}
						}
						any_owned_state = { is_core_of = CAN }
						NOT = { original_tag = CAN }
					}
					every_owned_state = {
						limit = { is_core_of = CAN }
						IF = { limit = { is_fully_controlled_by = OWNER } transfer_state_to = ROOT }
						ELSE = { set_state_owner_to = ROOT }
					}
				}
				every_country = {
					limit = {
						is_subject_of = ROOT
						any_owned_state = { is_core_of = CAN }
						original_tag = CAN
					}
					ROOT = {
						annex_country = {
							target = PREV
							transfer_troops = yes
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}

					}

		continue_if_invalid = no
		available_if_capitulated = no
		search_filters = {  }

		completion_reward = {
			IF = {
				limit = { is_subject = no is_faction_leader = yes }
				every_other_country = {
					limit = {
						has_country_flag = AZR_is_anti_col_com_rebel
						is_subject = no
						has_government = communism
						is_in_faction = no
						has_capitulated = no
					}
					ROOT = { add_to_faction = PREV }
				}
			}
			ELSE_IF = {
				limit = { is_subject = no is_in_faction = yes is_faction_leader = no }
				every_other_country = {
					limit = {
						has_country_flag = AZR_is_anti_col_com_rebel
						is_subject = no
						has_government = communism
						is_in_faction = no
						has_capitulated = no
					}
					ROOT = {
						set_autonomy = {
							target = PREV
							autonomy_state = autonomy_dominion
							end_wars = no
							end_civil_wars = no
						}
					}
				}
			}
			ELSE_IF = {
				limit = { is_subject = no is_in_faction = no }
				create_faction = azr_east_asian_independence_league
				every_other_country = {
					limit = {
						has_country_flag = AZR_is_anti_col_com_rebel
						is_subject = no
						has_government = communism
						is_in_faction = no
						has_capitulated = no
						has_civil_war = yes
					}
					ROOT = {
						add_to_faction = PREV
					}
				}
			}
			ELSE_IF = {
				limit = { is_subject = yes }
				effect_tooltip = { create_faction = azr_east_asian_independence_league }
				custom_effect_tooltip = AST_liberate_the_south_pacific_tt_2
			}
		}
	}
